# Phase 8: documentation reconciliation and integrated acceptance

[Index](HARBINGER_PROGRESSION_REDESIGN.md) · Run focused checks during each phase, then integrate here.

## Documentation to reconcile

### 2026-10-01 Circus observation mode correction

- `CircusDiscoveryProgress.onPlayerTick` previously treated spectators and dead players inside a pavilion as visitors, recording discovery and passive acclimation. It now checks living/non-spectator eligibility before structure observation, synchronizes outside perception on rejection and leaves saved proof untouched. Early living D0/inactive-blood discovery remains valid; referral, generation, routes, challenges, outcomes and Main gates are unchanged.
- Added `circusObservationRequiresLivingNonSpectator`: a supplied registered Circus structure start/reference with a fortress-piece bounding box is confirmed through the real structure query. Direct ticks verify spectator/dead rejection, living D0/inactive-blood discovery plus one passive point without rank credit, and preservation of earned proof without more points under spectator inspection. Original starts and deep-copied reference sets are restored; the actor is discarded. This is supplied loaded observation evidence, not a generated Circus, native travel, restart or death-clone acceptance.
- First `build/circus-observation-red-current.log` stopped at fixture API/degree compile errors. After correction, `build/circus-observation-red-confirmed-current.log` ran 130 clinical cases with exactly the new spectator assertion failing. The first green attempt passed the exclusions but failed the fixture's nullable named-degree enum assertion at D0 (`build/circus-observation-green-current.log`); it now uses `getDegreeNumber()`. Neither fixture error is claimed as a production fix. Scoped read-only review found no actionable issues.
- Final `runClinicalGameTestServer test --tests '*Circus*' verifyGameTestResourceLog --console=plain` passed 130 clinical, 66 focused JVM and all 700 combined runtime cases plus the resource-log gate, terminal exit 0 (`build/circus-observation-verified-current.log`). All four runs are terminal. Mechanics reference and root/detailed agent guidance agree. No native client or full JVM/alpha rerun is claimed; sampling-policy resolution, natural discovery/pacing, prior-discovery cold reporting, full Circus routes and wider multiplayer acceptance remain open.

### 2026-10-01 Native earned Staff death recovery

- Copied stopped `HarbingerStaffD2_20260930` to separate `EarnedStaffDeath_20261001`, preserving the original. That inherited death's cause is not established by this run. Normal Respawn retained the bond and knowledge. Mapped cycling and use recalled one Staff, without a given Staff or bond write. The first use was still selected Projection and produced its utility; another cycle/normal offhand swap and confirmed Conjure Staff selection corrected this operator mistake. No mechanic fix is claimed.
- Supplied a high stone platform, lower landing pad, position and view direction in the disposable copy. Ordinary forward input stepped off it onto natural terrain at Y69; Minecraft applied 28 fall damage and logged `BookKitReview fell from a high place`. The real death screen showed zero health/empty inventory. Normal stop saved exact bond progress, D3 and equipped gourd state with no ordinary inventory (`build/staff-native-dead-player.dat`). No kill, health write or manual clone was used for this fresh death.
- A new client reopened to the death screen, retaining the bond, 3,782 handled blood and selected Conjure Staff. Normal Respawn returned an empty living inventory away from the corpse. The mapped action recalled exactly one Staff without corpse pickup, a reward command or new formation. Saved recovered NBT (`build/staff-native-recalled-player.dat`) retained exact Staff inventory plus bond, equipment and degree. Another full client restart retained exactly the same inventory, known/equipped/selected memories, Staff progress, gourd equipment and degree in final stopped NBT. This is recall of the base Staff, not recovery of dropped customized forms or fittings.
- All three sessions stopped normally, terminal exit 0: `build/staff-native-death-current.log`, `build/staff-native-dead-reopen-current.log`, and `build/staff-native-recalled-cold-current.log`. Live client/server snapshots use `build/staff-native-{predeath,dead,cold-dead,respawn-recalled,recalled-cold}-` prefixes. Inspected screenshots are `staff-native-earned-death_png.png` and `staff-native-respawn-recalled.png` under `build/book-kit-client/screenshots`; the latter shows one Staff and otherwise empty ordinary inventory. Commands use `staff-death-` in the operator journal.
- The copied world inherits assisted rank, utility knowledge, earlier ingredients/formation setup and earned bond/gourd. Fresh terrain/travel/view direction isolate environmental death; no natural acquisition, fair combat, transformed/fitted Staff recovery, broad equipment behavior or multiplayer is established. No production code or suite gate reran. Prior tests remain historical, full alpha remains red on the unresolved sampling-policy choice, and broad redesign acceptance stays open.

### 2026-10-01 Full alpha refresh after supply recovery

- Fresh `alphaCheck --console=plain` completed all 2,408 JVM cases with exactly one failure, `BloodProfileMigrationTest.shipsExactlyTheApprovedRestrictions` (`build/progression-recovery-alpha-current.log`, terminal exit 1). The default gate stopped before runtime/resource tasks. No new JVM regression appeared after the First Draws and First Separation recovery changes.
- Fresh `alphaCheck --continue --console=plain` retained that same JVM failure while allowing all 700 required combined runtime cases and the strict resource-log verifier to pass (`build/progression-recovery-alpha-continue-current.log`, terminal exit 1 solely on `:test`). Both sessions are terminal. Clinical remains opt-in and was not rerun by this alpha command; its latest separate evidence is the preceding 116-case pass. No source changed during this refresh. The earlier zero-damage failure remains preserved; this passing run does not establish or fix its cause.
- A structured current-profile/fixture comparison isolates the policy difference: profiles restrict `hemomancy:armadillo`, `hemomancy:desiccant`, `hemomancy:mortarbound` and `hemomancy:naeglerophaeon`, and allow `hemomancy:chitinite`; the approved fixture expects the opposite for those five IDs. - Source review found a separate unverified interaction for the next recovery test: observational Chamber visits restore their entry inventory but not the pending-supply claims newly cleared by insertion. Retry during a temporary inventory change could consume a claim whose delivered item is discarded at return. This is a testable risk, not a reproduced failure or implemented correction yet. Natural Main-only flow/pacing, native pending/death/cold recovery, multiplayer and broad acceptance remain open; the policy question does not prevent other in-scope work.

### 2026-10-01 Pending first Spine delivery at death and Creative-full inventory

- `QliphothBloomEvents.deliverPendingFungalSpine` previously marked delivery from `Inventory.add`'s Creative-discard success and could insert into a dead player's cleared inventory before respawn discarded it. It now rejects dead-player retries and records delivery only when a Spine is actually carried. ## Source checks

Run narrow searches for the touched obsolete wording and references. Review matches in context; many legacy registry IDs must remain.

- Vicar's Consecration Kit: display text changes; vicars_consecration_kit ID and old claim compatibility remain.
- Seven tendencies / generic Fungal tendency: normal tendency count is eight.
- First Bloodcraft 5000 ml: current proof is 500 ml absorbed; distinguish vessel capacity and other unrelated costs.
- Concentrated Blood grants D2: target/current promotion is D2 -> D3.
- Somatic Loom or Reliquary first unlocked D4: actual gates remain D3.
- Anchorite first available D5: first scar chapter remains D4.
- Chamber first exists D6: distinguish early dreams/seat practice from D6 attunement.
- Fungal Gardens exclusively a dimension: preserve the explicit Overworld biome.
- Any sapling becomes bloodwood: target supports Dead Bush.
- Ordinary cylinders require ambergris: update all ordinary consumers, not ancient/specialty content.
- Saints, Canon acquisition or Drudges mandatory in alpha: keep deferred.

Check resource JSON, recipe IDs, translation keys, new item model/texture references, assignment serialization, packet field order, and every new Markdown link.

## Focused automated checks

Choose tests based on actual changed behavior. Favor existing fixtures and observable outcomes over new tests that merely search source text. Registry-backed interactions need a mod-loaded server.

The suite catalog is [gradle/game-tests.gradle](../../gradle/game-tests.gradle); [TESTING.md](../TESTING.md) explains launchers and run directories. Verify task availability again at execution time.

Typical compile/resource check:

    ./gradlew.bat compileJava compileGameTestJava processResources

Examples of existing focused commands:

    ./gradlew.bat test --tests '*FirstBloodcraftLedgerProgressTest' --tests '*FirstSeparationLedgerProgressTest' --tests '*FirstSeparationSpinProofTest' --tests '*HarbingerChapterMilestoneTest' --tests '*ClinicalBloodProgressTest'
    ./gradlew.bat runClinicalGameTestServer runBloodInjectionGameTestServer runDistillationGameTestServer
    ./gradlew.bat runScriptoriumGameTestServer runAdvancedBrewingGameTestServer
    ./gradlew.bat runAntecedentGameTestServer

Do not assume the combined suite includes every focused namespace. Current clinical, advanced-brewing, Scriptorium, Antecedent and several encounter suites are opt-in. For a new namespace, update DevTestHooks/catalog/template registration and run verifyGameTestSuites; do not create unregistered tests that never execute.

At integration, run the repository's required progression gate:

    ./gradlew.bat alphaCheck

Run additional focused suites that alphaCheck excludes when this work touches them. Record exact failures and whether the failing code changed. Never convert a partially green run into a full pass claim.

## Runtime acceptance matrix

| Scenario | Required evidence |
|---|---|
| Fresh Main-only player | D0 through D7 without optional catalogue completion, full armor sets, Circus, rare expeditions, Saints, or unavailable teachers. |
| Early scientist | D1 direct-vial samples survive and count at D2; restricted profiles still need syringe; centrifuge and personal extraction proofs work. |
| Out-of-order explorer | Early staff, crude memory, chair, reef/Deep Dark discovery, and station crafting are acknowledged without revoked access or duplicate rewards. |
| D3 memory route | Actual blank memory plus first weave produced before D4 cultivation or D5 incubation; material hints are sufficient without commands. |
| Routine cylinder user | Ordinary audio and basic/Precision Forge work require no ambergris; legacy ancient/master data survive. |
| Chamber learner | Explicit guided visit, direct chair entry, sleeping chair entry, timed return, and independent rite behave as documented. |
| Scar apprentice | D4 scar/effigy sequence is achievable and alone opens the D5 chapter gate. |
| Solo founder | Legitimate bloodline/Fane/recruit route supplies a valid Vigil helper without another human player. |
| Two players | Proof ownership, NPC event validation, distinct Chamber inventories/cells, founder membership, and rite ownership do not cross-credit. |
| Existing save | Old degree/claims, pending rest, completed weave/scars, Fane, Chamber attunement, loadouts, cylinders, and ending flags survive. |
| Recovery | Interrupt, die, log out, restart, fill inventory/output, and retry at each changed state boundary. |
| Endings | From legitimate pre-choice state, separately complete refusal and Apotheosis without cross-granting status/rewards. |

Use disposable test worlds/copies for recovery and endings. Do not delete or overwrite the user's runtime worlds. A fixture may accelerate reaching a late test state; label it as a fixture and separately record the survival acquisition path.

## Live flow review

- Read the first option and next-step text at every rank. The player should know what advances them, what is optional, where to go, and why.
- Check that degree unlocks do not present four equally urgent quest walls. Prefer the Main continuation, one contextual referral, and accessible optional branches.
- Verify cross-NPC referrals acknowledge completed work and shared observations.
- Check that recipe hints show actual catalysts, mediums, costs, floors, required staff, supported containers, and replacement routes.
- Time introductory lessons and trips. Record excessive material grind, repeated empty nights, duplicate travel, or text that reveals more than the player has earned.
- Inspect in-world items, ledger, dialogue, cylinder models and Chamber presentation. Automated source checks cannot approve these views.

## Phase handoff record

### 2026-10-01 Time-sliced Voyager charts

- Root cause: the local Minecraft `ChunkGenerator` locator loops through placement regions in one call and can wait for structure-start chunks. Moving that whole call to an arbitrary worker would expose world/structure state to unsafe access. The new server-tick queue instead uses vanilla's spiral iterator and radius-zero locator for individual regions, with at most two probes per tick and a 4 ms budget checked between probes. It preserves the default 100-region-radius coverage but not the previous iteration order; the message promises a vessel candidate, not an absolute nearest guarantee.
- Lifecycle: eight distinct pending players maximum, duplicate acknowledgements without new jobs, actual logout/server-stop cleanup, and per-tick active-path/Overworld/alive/prior-proof cancellation. A cancelled request is not requeued after a nested locator wait. A default chart requires a single tagged structure with one random-spread placement; missing/unsupported layouts report unavailable rather than silently pretending no vessel exists. No quest proof, off-thread world access, custom chunk ticket or forced chunk was added.
- Delivery review: capacity counts the active request as well as queued requests. After a vanilla probe, delivery rechecks pending status, eligibility/proof and the current connected player identity, because a managed chunk wait can process other server work. The nested-wait logout/path-change edge was reviewed in source, not forced in runtime.
- Regression: `build/voyager-queued-bearing-red-current.log` failed the new loaded acknowledgement assertion before implementation. `build/voyager-queued-bearing-alpha-current.log` passed 685 combined and 86 clinical tests plus the resource verifier, with the same three JVM failures. Added final coverage exercises eight distinct requests, duplicate slot retention, ninth-request refusal, real logout-event release and successful retry. Both fixtures explicitly post logout during cleanup so they cannot leave chart jobs in neighboring tests.
- Native replay: reopened disposable `VoyagerBearing_20261001`, seed 42061002, with its supplied D3/blood/Vicar/platform setup and no observation/report proof. Normal Quest Work requests acknowledged in about 5-7 ms. Two pending snapshots advanced from server tick 6,708 to 7,229 while chart work remained unfinished. Normal shutdown interrupted the first search; a fresh client could request again. The second request at 10:46:20 UTC returned the known vessel `(-21584,-22496)` at 10:47:36 UTC. A repeated choice at 10:47:23 UTC acknowledged the existing search, followed by only one bearing result. The replay log reported no "Can't keep up" warning. Final player state still had no Voyager observation/report proof, and both clients saved/stopped normally with exit 0.
- Artifacts: `build/voyager-queued-bearing-client-current.log`, `build/voyager-queued-bearing-reopen-current.log`; pending snapshots `build/voyager-queued-bearing-pending-a-server.json`, `build/voyager-queued-bearing-pending-b-server.json`, `build/voyager-queued-bearing-retry-pending-server.json`; final `build/voyager-queued-bearing-result-server.json`. Inspected captures under `build/book-kit-client/screenshots/`: `voyager-queued-bearing-pending.png`, `voyager-queued-bearing-result.png`.
- Final gates: `build/voyager-queued-bearing-final-alpha-current.log` and the subsequent delivery-guard run `build/voyager-queued-bearing-delivery-alpha-current.log` each passed 686/686 combined GameTests, 86/86 clinical tests and the unchanged strict resource-log verifier. Both completed 2,395 JVM tests with the same three failures: `BloodFillPixelsTest.fullFillTopMatchesStaticVesselTextures`, `BloodFillPixelsTest.upperFrameShoulderDoesNotCoverFillWithSilverPixels`, `BloodProfileMigrationTest.shipsExactlyTheApprovedRestrictions`. Alpha exited 1 solely on `:test`. Scoped diff checks passed; no required client/Gradle process remained running.
- Limits: total chart time increased to about 76 seconds while ordinary server ticks continued; this is not a faster total search or a hard per-tick latency guarantee. One radius-zero probe can still block on vanilla worldgen/storage. The native replay preceded the final capacity-count/nested-cancellation review guards and does not test those edges; the final gate compiled this source but did not force nested-wait state changes. Full missing-candidate scans, unsupported charts, leave-dimension/death/path-change cancellation, real multi-player queue pressure, other seeds, natural travel and broader progression acceptance remain open.

## Completion rule

The cleanup is complete only when the implemented player journey, server gates, saved state, dialogue/ledger/recipes, references, and observed runtime journey agree. If code and automated tests are complete but live checks are unavailable, state that exact limit and leave the live acceptance items open.

## Verification of this documentation packet

This packet was prepared from a read-only gameplay-source audit of the current checkout on 2026-09-28, including its existing uncommitted progression changes. Ten new Markdown files were created. Three narrow .gitignore exceptions make this packet visible to Git despite the existing docs-wide ignore rule. No gameplay code, resources, existing lore/reference pages, or runtime worlds were changed for this request. Current-source checks established the promotion graph, relevant recipe gates, direct-vial path, syringe reward, first-weave ingredient route, Chamber modes, station tiers, kit claim path, and cylinder consumers.

Packet verification passed: 10 Markdown files, 45 local links, eight implementation phases, 22 current recipe gates, all six promotion certifications, and the chicken-profile prerequisite for Neurotic Enzyme. Markdown structure/whitespace checks and the scoped Git whitespace check passed. No Java build, GameTest, fresh-world survival journey, or multiplayer run was performed for this documentation-only request.

## 2026-09-28 Phase 7 Bloom lifecycle identity slice

- Behavior changed: Each Bloom has a saved lifecycle UUID. Fruit and its pending husk, dropped count, severed state, player Communion count, and Vesper attempt/phase now refer to that tree, so equal coordinates in two dimensions or a rebuilt tree cannot share progress. A Vesper loaded from an old attempt only seals a uniquely matching legacy Bloom.

- Save/compatibility handling: Old world Blooms receive UUIDs on load and retain a migration marker through later saves. Old position-keyed Bloom state and player progress move to that marked lifecycle; a fresh Bloom at the same site cannot claim them. Old picked fruit without a UUID uses the old position path when its original Bloom cannot be identified. Pre-ID state with two Blooms at identical coordinates in different dimensions was already ambiguous and can only be assigned to the first matching legacy entry.

## 2026-09-30 Phase 8 actual rest and dialogue navigation

- Behavior changed: Concentrated Blood and completed-sleep Chamber visits no longer treat the slow wake animation as proof of a completed night. Minecraft's Leave Bed packet calls `stopSleepInBed(false, true)`; the server's completed-night wake calls `stopSleepInBed(false, false)`. The handler now requires both wake flags to be false. Leaving bed or an immediate wake keeps the injection pending. Partly visible dialogue topics and options now accept mouse clicks only inside the content viewport, so clipped portions cannot intercept Back or header clicks.
- Developer harness: `hemo test journey harbinger run_main_to_rest` pauses the Main route before D2 injection or sleep. Both manual pause modes synchronize the selected hotbar slot. The initial live replay showed a client/server selection mismatch after server-side automation; synchronization removed the extra slot-change workaround in the fixed replay. These changes do not bypass production injection or sleep gates.
- Focused evidence: The existing Concentrated Blood GameTest now exercises `PlayerWakeUpEvent` with Leave Bed, immediate-wake, and completed-night flags. Before the handler fix, `runBloodInjectionGameTestServer --console=plain` completed 88 tests with this one required failure, "Injection granted degree early". Afterward all 88 passed. `test --tests '*Dialogue*' --console=plain` passed 73 tests, including clipping coverage. The current `runClinicalGameTestServer --console=plain` passed 43/43. Compilation and the scoped whitespace check passed.
- Live evidence: Disposable `HarbingerActualRest_20260930` first reproduced premature D3 promotion on Leave Bed. With the fix loaded, a reset Main route paused at D2; rendered Alchemist dialogue issued Concentrated Blood, and client item use recorded pending injection. A real bed interaction opened the Leave Bed screen. Leaving it kept D2 and pending rest. A full client shutdown and reopen retained both. Restoring normal night skipping and completing a later bed sleep awarded the D3 advancement and cleared pending rest without a second vial or a call to the synthetic sleep helper. Clearing restored D0 and removed the journey snapshot. The clipped Quest Work topic also stopped intercepting the Back button in the fixed client.
- Test assists and limits: The early route supplied materials and accelerated preceding actions. Peaceful difficulty removed monsters below the fixture bed. For the interrupted case, `playersSleepingPercentage=101` and a frozen night prevented the server from completing the night while the player was in bed; the completion case restored 100 percent and the daylight cycle. The disposable client's use key was rebound. These checks prove the observed sleep-event, pending-state persistence, and navigation behavior, not natural survival, ordinary control balance, multiplayer sleep thresholds, or the full Chamber dream matrix.
- Integrated result: The current `runGameTestServer --console=plain` completed 671 tests with nine required failures: `fullCorpusBindsOnDedicatedServer`, `threeAnchorHelpersRepairTwoActualRings`, `scarSupervisionIsLocalAndDoesNotGrantMastery`, `wardenPaysOnlyForALivingRiteThreat`, `naturalSpawnGatesAcceptBasinOvergrowth`, `obscuredMeleeMobCanPursueAndHitANearbyEnemy`, `firstRemnantJourneyUsesRealInscriptionAndRestoresKnowledge`, `veinwingVultureFlightControllerPursuesAssignedTarget`, and `veinwingVultureFiresAFeatherVolleyBeforeClosingToMelee`. The wake regression and to-choice pause test passed in that run. The Remnant test reported `NoSuchElementException`; its cause remains unproven. The integrated gate remains red.

## 2026-10-01 Phase 5 ticking item-query positive controls

- Scope correction: immediate item queries did not establish chunk readiness or natural entity ticking. The four restoration cases now hold UUID-owned transient tickets for the origin/private cell, wait for every chunk intersecting their eight-/sixteen-block query areas to become entity-loaded, spawn one non-pickup/invulnerable/no-gravity item probe per dimension, and require both probes to be visible before recording baselines. - Captured red: `build/chamber-ticking-item-queries-current.log` failed dream return at the five-natural-tick prerequisite. `build/chamber-item-probe-tick-diagnostic-current.log` reproduced it: the overworld probe had 193 ticks, the Chamber probe zero; both were alive and UUID-indexed. Earlier readiness and immediate restoration assertions had passed. These are fixture-readiness failures, not new production inventory failures.
- Root cause: mapped `ServerLevel.tick` stops the entity loop after the empty-dimension grace period unless it has players or saved forced chunks. `ForcedChunkManager.hasForcedChunks` reads saved chunk/controller forcing, not the transient region tickets. The test now adds one vanilla forced anchor at its new private cell, recording its prior forced state. Cleanup retains a pre-existing anchor and removes only one newly added by this fixture; transient tickets still cover query-area loading. Production dimension tick behavior is unchanged.
- Cleanup: pass/failure listeners discard the exact actor and two probes, clear fixture cursor/crafting/inventory copies, release the UUID-owned tickets in both levels, and remove the newly added anchor. Existing items are never deleted. Total timeout is 200 ticks for readiness and the delayed observation; no manual entity ticks are used. Failure/timeout cleanup with the new anchor, a pre-existing forced anchor, command cancellation, and hard-crash cleanup have not been separately exercised.
- Fixed loaded observations: `runBookKitReviewClient -PchamberLoadedValidation=true --console=plain`, `build/chamber-ticking-item-queries-fixed-current.log`, reopened only disposable `ChamberLoadedEntry_20261001`. Dream return passed at 04:06:51, dream recovery at 04:07:14, guided return at 04:07:28, and guided recovery at 04:07:44. Each completed independently and required both probes to receive at least five natural ticks, query chunks still loaded, and unchanged UUID sets. The before/after `/execute in hemomancy:chamber_of_will run forceload query` commands at 04:06:50 and 04:07:48 both reported no forced chunks. Normal stop saved the disposable world and exited zero.
- Default verification: `runClinicalGameTestServer --console=plain`, `build/chamber-ticking-default-clinical-current.log`, exited zero with all 86 required tests passing. Compilation and suite-registration verification also ran through the client/server launchers. Scoped whitespace checks passed.
- Limits: this proves a bounded loaded-window item query around the origin/private cell under fixture-held ticking conditions. It does not establish genuine unload/reload persistence, long-duration drops, saved-world crash recovery, native rendered-menu behavior, natural progression, or multiplayer. The three existing entry-guard bodies and opt-in registration are unchanged. ## 2026-09-30 Phase 4 Staff selection, blood eligibility, and base persistence

- Confirmed Staff selector defect: `LivingStaffItem.isSelectedStaffUtility` read the legacy Noetic selector, which intentionally remains unchanged under a shared Thelemic selection. The new absorption/projection checks failed exactly at the stale utility assertion in `build/staff-selection-red-current.log` (52 tests, two required failures). The helper now matches the shared entry's MANIPULATION kind and utility ID. Returning to the selected utility restores held use; unrelated shared Noetic entries do not inherit it. Bonds, forms, fittings, the D1 recipe gate, and other Staff interactions are unchanged.
- Confirmed early-rite defect: inactive blood started Rooted Vein and escrowed its Staff. The first red command also found a test package typo, so its server suite did not run; the lesson regression did fail as expected. After correcting that typo, `build/staff-eligibility-red-confirmed-current.log` completed 53 tests with the sole expected inactive-start failure. The new guard runs in the matched positive-degree Harbinger branch before rite creation or escrow. Unstained rules are unchanged; the two current degree-zero recipes are Unstained. Degree-zero preservation was source-reviewed, not a new runtime proof.
- Regression coverage: the Rooted Vein fixture verifies inactive rejection without losing the held Staff or spending blood, D1 rejection despite Staff ownership, and successful active D2 retry on the same floor/offering. The existing blocked-Bloom fixture now activates blood so it still reaches its obstruction/retry assertions. The Artificer lesson names Conjure Staff recall and selecting Blood Absorption or Blood Projection. Its new JVM assertion first failed at the missing selection instruction.
- Focused green: `runClinicalGameTestServer runLivingWeaponGameTest runStationUpgradeGameTestServer test --tests '*LivingStaff*' --tests '*EquipmentLessonDialogueTest' --console=plain` passed 53 clinical, eight Living Weapon, and 32 station-upgrade GameTests, plus 18 discovered JUnit tests (11 Staff morph/claw tests, six equipment lesson tests, and one Cardinal activation test matched by its method name). The other Staff classes matched by that glob are standalone legacy tests, not JUnit executions. Log: `build/staff-eligibility-green-current.log`. A read-only reviewer found no scoped correctness issues; actual utility pulse coverage is provided by the separate native replay below.
- Native construction: new disposable Survival world `HarbingerStaffD2_20260930` used assisted D2, active blood, protection, a platform, Artificer spawn, formation blocks, and a Sanguine Formation offhand catalyst. The first platform command targeted unloaded terrain and failed; the corrected loaded platform and relocation were used for the checks. The initial probe note screen and failed cycling before utility knowledge were fixture setup, not lesson/crafting proofs. Absorption and Projection knowledge were supplied explicitly because the degree command does not teach them. Normal Artificer conversation displayed the D2 construction/use lesson. Normal mapped Projection conjuration and 180 held-use ticks completed the authored Staff formation, consumed its catalyst, and earned `livingStaffBond=true`; the Staff and bond were not command-given. The formation's authored cost is 150 mL; the live run did not isolate that exact debit from conjuration/regeneration.
- Native utility use: normal Conjure Staff produced the earned Staff. With Projection selected, held use began facing air, then ordinary aim moved onto a supplied Alembic. This avoids its normal menu-opening interaction while exercising the Staff's usual held channel. The Staff projected 1,891 mL into its reservoir and then, with Absorption selected, drew the same amount back. Its committed handled-blood total reached 3,782 mL. Inactive blood subsequently left that total at 3,782 and the player reservoir at 1,949 mL through another held Projection attempt. Snapshots: `build/staff-d2-projection.json`, `build/staff-d2-absorption.json`, and `build/staff-d2-inactive-projection.json`. All accepted replay snapshots were living players on the world screen.
- Cold persistence: full client shutdown/reopen retained D2, the base Staff in slot 3, the bond, 3,782 mL handled, inactive blood, and selected Projection. ## 2026-09-30 Phase 7/8 helper fixture readiness

- Root cause: `build/rite-helper-diagnostic-current.log` completed 673 tests with seven required failures. The three helper failures reported `indexed=false` and `entitiesLoaded=false`, despite living, recruited, unspent NPCs, stone footing, and zero block/entity collisions. The small vanilla template does not ensure that the offset helper stations are entity-loaded. These were fixture availability failures, not evidence of incorrect production eligibility.
- Fix: `HarbingerRepairGameTests` adds per-actor UUID ticking tickets for the station chunks, waits for `areEntitiesLoaded`, then checks the original helper availability and effects. The Attendant must reject 45 mL without spending and accept exactly 50 mL once; the Warden must spend nothing on a missing threat and exactly 25 mL on a living threat; fractional shared blood must remain intact; three Anchors must deliver 30 mL to the real rings and retain 2,970 mL in private reserves. No production rule was relaxed. Tickets, actors, NPCs, and bloodlines are cleaned up on normal completion, assertion failure, or readiness timeout.
- Timing evidence: A fixed two-tick delay still caught the Warden before chunk activation in `build/rite-helper-ticket-current.log` (673 tests, four required failures). It was replaced by an actual readiness sequence, not a longer arbitrary delay.
- Repeated evidence: `runGameTestServer --console=plain` completed 673 tests in each of `build/rite-helper-loaded-current.log`, `build/rite-helper-loaded-repeat-current.log`, and `build/rite-helper-final-current.log`. All four helper checks reported no failures at the three different test origins. These runs still had three, five, and five other required failures respectively. The final run includes the failure-cleanup listener fix; a read-only review found no remaining issues. The timeout callback was reviewed structurally, not proven with a forced readiness timeout.
- Final required failures: `naturalSpawnGatesAcceptBasinOvergrowth`, `naturalSpawnAcceptsSafeGroundNearOvergrowth`, `mnemonistSurvivesDaylightAndPursuesItsTarget`, `firstRemnantJourneyUsesRealInscriptionAndRestoresKnowledge` (missing report Vicar), and `veinwingVultureFiresAFeatherVolleyBeforeClosingToMelee`. Failure sets vary across these origins; no new cause is inferred for the remaining tests. Body Answers and Carousel did not fail in the final run, which does not establish their intermittent causes are fixed.
- Scoped checks: `compileJava compileGameTestJava processResources --console=plain` and scoped `git diff --check` passed. Compile log: `build/rite-helper-final-compile-current.log`. No client or required Gradle process was left running.
- Limits: This is server-fixture validation, not live travel, natural recruitment, full Covenant Vigil, saved-world recovery, or multiplayer acceptance. The JVM suite was not rerun for this test-only change; the latest full result remains the separately documented 2,371 tests with 19 failures. The integrated gate is still red.

## 2026-09-30 Phase 3 paid-weave recovery and learned-memory handoff

- Completion after interruption: In the same disposable `HarbingerFirstWeave_20260930`, normal Loom interactions loaded supplied blanks/bulbs and 20 additional enzymes. The second weave saved with 50 mL paid, an unfinished Animus strand, and 21 stored enzymes. A full client shutdown and reopen retained the recipe, commitment, blank, bulb, blood payment, and strand. Normal held-Staff input caught it and completed crystallization at 18:25:04 UTC, leaving 20 enzymes. Drawing it home used an assisted relocation for the captured depth; no second Projection payment was needed. The result crystallized, but this second output was not collected as a separate reward-persistence proof.
- Confirmed bug and fix: Holding Projection after payment repeated the full Staff instructions every tick and filled the rendered chat. `SomaticLoomBlockEntity.tryChargeRitualBlood` now returns quietly for a valid orb-weaving state after recipe refresh. Invalid saved commitments still report recovery; explicit empty-hand use still explains the controls. The first test attempt captured the wrong packet path and failed its explicit-help assertion; its fixture was corrected to capture `displayClientMessage`. The confirmed red run then failed at "Holding Projection after payment must not repeat Staff instructions every tick". The green `runClinicalGameTestServer --console=plain` passed 44/44, including the single-payment, explicit-help, and missing-recipe checks. Logs: `build/loom-feedback-red-current.log`, `build/loom-feedback-red-confirmed-current.log`, and `build/loom-feedback-green-current.log`. A read-only scoped review found no issues.
- Fixed replay and cancellation: A fixed client held Projection for 60 normal ticks after selecting the third Blood Shot weave. It scattered the strand once without repeated instructions. Held-Staff input spent two enzymes before shutdown. Reopening retained 50 mL paid, the unfinished strand with two spent enzymes, the blank/bulb, and 18 unspent enzymes. Normal empty-hand use showed help once. Crouch-use cancelled; the next two crouch-uses returned the bulb and blank to inventory. The Loom retained 18 enzymes and no strand, selection, commitment, or paid charge. This checks input and enzyme accounting; passive blood regeneration prevented an exact live before/after refund measurement.
- Learned-memory handoff: The original first-weave Blood Shot was offered to an Iron Brazier, lit with normal Projection, and learned after 60 ticks of normal Blood Absorption. The supplied Absorption item was held for 100 ticks, so its post-completion continuation also harmed a nearby fixture chicken; that is not learning evidence. A Reliquary formation was supplied, but its 75 mL craft used normal Projection with the formation in the offhand. The personally crafted item was placed normally and opened without bypassing station access. Its rendered screen showed Blood Shot equipped at 1/4 slots. Normal Mnemonist interaction recognized the first weave and awarded `memory_weaving/memory_conductive_mark`. No learned manipulation or recognition advancement was injected.
- Final cold reload: A further full shutdown and reopen retained the learned/equipped Blood Shot, the placed Reliquary and its personal-access evidence, the Conductive Mark recipe, the recovered blank/bulb, and 18 Loom enzymes. The Reliquary tooltip identified Blood Shot as an equipped Noetic Memory. Repeating normal Mnemonist interaction did not repeat the recognition message. All client processes exited before the integrated Gradle run.
- Documentation: The mechanics reference and Harbinger wiki now distinguish full-memory brazier absorption from direct-use crude shards, describe explicit output choice and fixed-distance Staff handling, and explain paid-weave cancellation/recovery. Phase 3 and the index record these assisted checks without marking the phase complete.
- Final scoped checks: `compileJava compileGameTestJava processResources --console=plain` passed after the integrated run, and scoped `git diff --check` passed. Log: `build/loom-recovery-final-compile-current.log`. No client or required Gradle process was left running.
- Integrated evidence: After all clients stopped, `alphaCheck --continue --console=plain` completed 2,371 JVM tests with 19 failures and 673 GameTests with six required failures: `threeAnchorHelpersRepairTwoActualRings`, `wardenPaysOnlyForALivingRiteThreat`, `helperPaymentDoesNotRoundFractionalPoolIntoFreeBlood`, `mnemonistSurvivesDaylightAndPursuesItsTarget`, `carouselSpawnsThreeBoundPuppeteerCaptives`, and `veinwingVultureFiresAFeatherVolleyBeforeClosingToMelee`. The first three failed because supplied helpers were unavailable before their effects could be measured. Existing paid-Loom reload and journey tests reported no failures. Clinical validation remains a separate opt-in suite. The JVM failure set remains the 19 failures listed in the earlier gate notes; no cause is inferred from the Loom change. The integrated gate is not green, and its dependent resource-error check could not complete. Log: `build/loom-recovery-alpha-current.log`.
